# 2027 Software Engineering Internship & New Grad Positions

This repository is a comprehensive list of Software Engineering jobs for college students in search of **internships** or **new graduate** positions. The positions are updated daily, and we prioritize jobs posted within the last 120 days.

### USA Positions :eagle:
- [Internships :books:](/) - **1042** available ([FAANG+](#faang), [Quant](#quant), [Other](#other))
- [New Graduate :mortar_board:](/NEW_GRAD_USA.md) - **1196** available ([FAANG+](/NEW_GRAD_USA.md#faang), [Quant](/NEW_GRAD_USA.md#quant), [Other](/NEW_GRAD_USA.md#other))

### International Positions :globe_with_meridians:
- [Internships :books:](/INTERN_INTL.md) - **814** available ([FAANG+](/INTERN_INTL.md#faang), [Quant](/INTERN_INTL.md#quant), [Other](/INTERN_INTL.md#other))
- [New Graduate :mortar_board:](/NEW_GRAD_INTL.md) - **1117** available ([FAANG+](/NEW_GRAD_INTL.md#faang), [Quant](/NEW_GRAD_INTL.md#quant), [Other](/NEW_GRAD_INTL.md#other))
